- ★ **2024-11-08 20:20** · Michael _{Canon/Bio: Jagwar Twin & Roy origin story (Michael)}_ — Roy = I was in highschool. My friend and drummer in my band's dad owned Chain Reaction a local music venue. I hung out there all the time and Roys band played one night. I remember watching him and thinking "this guy is either on drugs or he knows something that I want to know" and then just started hanging out with him and became friends.  ⏎ Jagwar Twin = in 2018 (i think) we were in Lake Arrowhead, CA at a cabin writing for his next album, at the time I was focusing on being a songwriter and music producer. He was writing stuff that sounded nothing like his other music and I remember us sitting around the fire trying to come up with names for this next project, I don't think Loser was written yet. One of the names he liked the most was "Rat Boy" hahah but I'm glad he didn't go with that one. The next morning he woke up and wrote Long Time Coming and then Matt the producer said this feels more of a Rock project it should have a more Rock feeling name. And then Jagwar Twin was born. Been a fan ever since, actually ended up writing some of the stuff on that first album.  ([↗](https://discord.com/channels/705391219915882516/709750776284708955/1304541173972729876))
- **2024-11-08 20:25** · Michael — #RatBoy  ([↗](https://discord.com/channels/705391219915882516/709750776284708955/1304542241255325706))

- ★ **2025-11-16 06:17** · **Roy** _{the-circle: 'not your homie' Spotify mission (Roy)}_ — Hey beautiful humans. I need your help with something so I have a little mission for us... ⏎ not your homie is 2:48 long. ⏎ An 8-hour overnight playlist equals about 171 plays. ⏎ That’s 1,197 plays in a week, 5,130 in a month, and over 30,000 in six months from just one person. ⏎ Now imagine even just 20 of us in The Circle doing it together ⏎ That’s over 100,000 plays in a month, enough to juice the algorithm, lift the song, and show the label that people care about Jagwar Twin and it deserves a bigger push. This really is a pivotal moment. ⏎ So the mission is... ⏎ Make a playlist on Spotify ⏎ Add not your homie 171 or more time (enough times to last through the night) ⏎ Save it. Listen during your day or while you sleep. Loudly or quietly. Share it if you feel inspired. And we will launch this! ⏎ I'm so grateful for you guys and everything we're building here in this little world. I'll be doing this every night. Who's in?
